############################################################
FILE: .\src\main.ts
############################################################
import { NestFactory } from "@nestjs/core";
import { ValidationPipe } from "@nestjs/common";
import helmet from "helmet";
import * as compression from "compression";
import { AppModule } from "./app.module";

async function bootstrap() {
  const app = await NestFactory.create(AppModule);

  app.use(helmet());
  app.use(compression());

  app.enableCors({
    origin: true,
    credentials: true,
  });

  app.useGlobalPipes(
    new ValidationPipe({
      whitelist: true,
      transform: true,
      forbidNonWhitelisted: false,
    }),
  );

  await app.listen(process.env.PORT ? Number(process.env.PORT) : 3000);
}

bootstrap();


############################################################
FILE: .\prisma\schema.prisma
############################################################
generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "sqlite"
  url      = "file:./dev.db"
}

model User {
  id           String   @id @default(cuid())
  email        String   @unique
  passwordHash String
  name         String?
  role         Role     @default(USER)
  createdAt    DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([role])
}

model Organization {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  name      String
  slug      String   @unique
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
  branches  Branch[]
}

model Branch {
  id             String       @id @default(cuid())
  name           String
  city           String?
  organizationId String
  createdAt      DateTime     @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime     @updatedAt
  organization   Organization @relation(fields: [organizationId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@index([organizationId])
}

model Vehicle {
  id                 String              @id @default(cuid())
  vin                String              @unique
  make               String
  model              String
  year               Int
  color              String?
  status             String              @default("AVAILABLE")
  location           String?
  brandId            String?
  vehicleModelId     String?
  trimId             String?
  dealerId           String?
  showroomId         String?
  createdAt          DateTime            @default(now())
  updatedAt          DateTime            @updatedAt
  inventory          Inventory?
  maintenanceRecords MaintenanceRecord[]
  serviceRecords     ServiceRecord[]
  testDrives         TestDrive[]
  showroom           Showroom?           @relation(fields: [showroomId], references: [id])
  dealer             Dealer?             @relation(fields: [dealerId], references: [id])
  trim               VehicleTrim?        @relation(fields: [trimId], references: [id])
  vehicleModel       VehicleModel?       @relation(fields: [vehicleModelId], references: [id])
  brand              VehicleBrand?       @relation(fields: [brandId], references: [id])

  @@index([make])
  @@index([model])
  @@index([year])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([location])
  @@index([brandId])
  @@index([vehicleModelId])
  @@index([trimId])
  @@index([dealerId])
  @@index([showroomId])
}

model VehicleBrand {
  id        String         @id @default(cuid())
  name      String         @unique
  country   String?
  isActive  Boolean        @default(true)
  createdAt DateTime       @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime       @updatedAt
  vehicles  Vehicle[]
  models    VehicleModel[]
}

model VehicleModel {
  id        String        @id @default(cuid())
  brandId   String
  name      String
  bodyType  String?
  isActive  Boolean       @default(true)
  createdAt DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime      @updatedAt
  vehicles  Vehicle[]
  brand     VehicleBrand  @relation(fields: [brandId], references: [id])
  trims     VehicleTrim[]

  @@index([brandId])
}

model VehicleTrim {
  id        String       @id @default(cuid())
  modelId   String
  name      String
  engine    String?
  gearbox   String?
  fuelType  String?
  createdAt DateTime     @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime     @updatedAt
  vehicles  Vehicle[]
  model     VehicleModel @relation(fields: [modelId], references: [id])

  @@index([modelId])
}

model Inventory {
  id           String          @id @default(cuid())
  vehicleId    String          @unique
  warehouseId  String?
  status       String          @default("AVAILABLE")
  price        Float?
  location     String?
  stockNumber  String?         @unique
  reserved     Boolean         @default(false)
  reservedAt   DateTime?
  receivedAt   DateTime?
  notes        String?
  createdAt    DateTime        @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime        @updatedAt
  warehouse    Warehouse?      @relation(fields: [warehouseId], references: [id])
  vehicle      Vehicle         @relation(fields: [vehicleId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  reservations Reservation[]
  movements    StockMovement[]

  @@index([vehicleId])
  @@index([warehouseId])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([location])
  @@index([reserved])
  @@index([stockNumber])
}

model Customer {
  id           String        @id @default(cuid())
  name         String
  email        String?
  phone        String?
  createdAt    DateTime      @default(now())
  updatedAt    DateTime      @updatedAt
  contracts    Contract[]
  invoices     Invoice[]
  orders       Order[]
  payments     Payment[]
  quotes       Quote[]
  reservations Reservation[]
  sales        Sale[]
  salesOrders  SalesOrder[]
  testDrives   TestDrive[]

  @@index([email])
  @@index([phone])
}

model Supplier {
  id             String          @id @default(cuid())
  name           String
  email          String?
  status         SupplierStatus  @default(ACTIVE)
  address        String?
  phone          String?
  createdAt      DateTime        @default(now())
  updatedAt      DateTime        @updatedAt
  purchaseOrders PurchaseOrder[]
}

model Lead {
  id        String   @id @default(cuid())
  name      String
  phone     String?
  source    String?
  status    String   @default("NEW")
  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([status])
  @@index([source])
}

model Order {
  id         String    @id @default(cuid())
  number     String?   @unique
  status     String    @default("DRAFT")
  total      Float     @default(0)
  notes      String?
  customerId String?
  quoteId    String?   @unique
  createdAt  DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt  DateTime  @updatedAt
  invoice    Invoice?
  quote      Quote?    @relation(fields: [quoteId], references: [id])
  customer   Customer? @relation(fields: [customerId], references: [id])
  payments   Payment[]
  sale       Sale?

  @@index([status])
  @@index([customerId])
}

model Payment {
  id         String    @id @default(cuid())
  amount     Float
  method     String?
  reference  String?
  status     String    @default("PENDING")
  paidAt     DateTime?
  notes      String?
  customerId String?
  orderId    String?
  invoiceId  String?
  createdAt  DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt  DateTime  @updatedAt
  invoice    Invoice?  @relation(fields: [invoiceId], references: [id])
  order      Order?    @relation(fields: [orderId], references: [id])
  customer   Customer? @relation(fields: [customerId], references: [id])

  @@index([status])

model PersistentAuditEvent {
  id                 String    @id @default(cuid())
  sequence           Int       @unique
  eventType          String
  severity           String
  action             String
  message            String
  actor              String?
  correlationId      String?
  traceId            String?
  method             String?
  path               String?
  statusCode         Int?
  metadata           Json?
  previousHash       String
  hash               String    @unique
  signature          String?
  signatureAlgorithm String?
  signatureKeyId     String?
  signedAt           DateTime?
  createdAt          DateTime  @default(now())

  @@index([eventType])
  @@index([severity])
  @@index([actor])
  @@index([correlationId])
